In practice, however, human preferences are inherently pluralistic, and aligned models must balance multiple downstream objectives, such as aesthetic quality and text-image consistency. Existing multi-objective approaches either rely on costly multi-objective RL fine-tuning or on fusing separately aligned models at denoising time, but they generally require access to reward values (or their gradients) and/or introduce approximation error in the resulting denoising objectives. In this paper, we revisit the problem of RL fine-tuning for diffusion models and address the intractability of identifying the optimal policy by introducing a step-level RL formulation. Building on this, we further propose Multi-objective Step-level Denoising-time Diffusion Alignment (MSDDA), a retraining-free framework for aligning diffusion models with multiple objectives, obtaining the optimal reverse denoising distribution in closed form, with mean and variance expressed directly in terms of single-objective base models. We prove that this denoising-time objective is exactly equivalent to the step-level RL fine-tuning, introducing no approximation error. Moreover, we provide numerical results, which indicate our method outperforms existing denoising-time approaches.

</details>


### [113] [Auxiliary Finite-Difference Residual-Gradient Regularization for PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14472)
*Stavros Kassinos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合PINN设计：PDE残差保持自动微分，有限差分仅用于惩罚残差场梯度的弱辅助项，以正则化残差场而不替换PDE残差本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN通常使用单一标量损失函数，即使关注的具体物理量更为特定。需要一种更针对性的方法，在保持自动微分优势的同时，利用有限差分进行特定正则化。

Method: 两阶段研究：阶段1为受控泊松基准测试，比较基线PINN、FD残差梯度正则器和匹配的AD残差梯度基线；阶段2将相同逻辑应用于三维环形热传导基准测试，在波浪形外壁附近实施贴体壳层网格作为辅助网格。

Result: 阶段1中FD正则器重现了残差梯度控制的主要效果，同时揭示了场精度与残差清洁度之间的权衡；阶段2中壳层正则器改善了外壁通量和边界条件行为等应用相关量。在Kourkoutas-beta优化器下，固定壳层权重5e-4是最可靠的配置，相比无壳层项，外壁BC RMSE从1.22e-2降至9.29e-4，壁通量RMSE从9.21e-3降至9.63e-4。

Conclusion: 辅助性FD正则器在与感兴趣的物理量（如外壁通量）对齐时最有价值，支持混合PINN的针对性观点：保持PDE残差的自动微分，仅对特定物理量使用有限差分正则化。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are often selected by a single scalar loss even when the quantity of interest is more specific. We study a hybrid design in which the governing PDE residual remains automatic-differentiation (AD) based, while finite differences (FD) appear only in a weak auxiliary term that penalizes gradients of the sampled residual field. The FD term regularizes the residual field without replacing the PDE residual itself.
  We examine this idea in two stages. Stage 1 is a controlled Poisson benchmark comparing a baseline PINN, the FD residual-gradient regularizer, and a matched AD residual-gradient baseline. Stage 2 transfers the same logic to a three-dimensional annular heat-conduction benchmark (PINN3D), where baseline errors concentrate near a wavy outer wall and the auxiliary grid is implemented as a body-fitted shell adjacent to the wall.
  In Stage 1, the FD regularizer reproduces the main effect of residual-gradient control while exposing a trade-off between field accuracy and residual cleanliness. In Stage 2, the shell regularizer improves the application-facing quantities, namely outer-wall flux and boundary-condition behavior. Across seeds 0-5 and 100k epochs, the most reliable tested configuration is a fixed shell weight of 5e-4 under the Kourkoutas-beta optimizer regime: relative to a matched run without the shell term, it reduces the mean outer-wall BC RMSE from 1.22e-2 to 9.29e-4 and the mean wall-flux RMSE from 9.21e-3 to 9.63e-4. Adam with beta2=0.999 becomes usable when the initial learning rate is reduced to 1e-3, although its shell benefit is less robust than under Kourkoutas-beta. Overall, the results support a targeted view of hybrid PINNs: an auxiliary-only FD regularizer is most valuable when it is aligned with the physical quantity of interest, here the outer-wall flux.

</details>


### [114] [Scouting By Reward: VLM-TO-IRL-Driven Player Selection For Esports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14474)
*Qing Yan,Wenyu Yang,Yufei Wang,Wenhao Ma,Linchong Hu,Yifei Jin,Anton Dahbura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于逆强化学习的电竞选手风格评估框架，通过学习职业选手的奖励函数来评估候选人与目标明星选手的战术风格匹配度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电竞选手评估方法依赖人工视频分析和聚合性能指标，难以捕捉细微的决策模式，无法准确判断候选人是否符合特定战术原型

Method: 采用逆强化学习框架，构建多模态双分支架构：一支编码高分辨率游戏遥测数据的结构化状态-动作轨迹，另一支编码视觉语言模型从比赛录像生成的战术伪解说；通过生成对抗模仿学习目标融合表示，判别器学习精英选手独特的机械和战术特征

Result: 框架实现了从通用技能评估到"基于奖励"的选拔转变，提供可扩展的工作流感知数字孪生系统，支持数据驱动的阵容构建和大规模候选人池的定向人才发现

Conclusion: 该框架通过逆强化学习将电竞选手风格评估重新定义为奖励函数学习问题，为组织提供了基于目标明星选手风格匹配度的候选人排名方法，解决了传统评估方法的局限性

Abstract: Traditional esports scouting workflows rely heavily on manual video review and aggregate performance metrics, which often fail to capture the nuanced decision-making patterns necessary to determine if a prospect fits a specific tactical archetype. To address this, we reframe style-based player evaluation in esports as an Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) problem. In this paper, we introduce a novel player selection framework that learns professional-specific reward functions from logged gameplay demonstrations, allowing organizations to rank candidates by their stylistic alignment with a target star player. Our proposed architecture utilizes a multimodal, two-branch intake: one branch encodes structured state-action trajectories derived from high-resolution in-game telemetry, while the second encodes temporally aligned tactical pseudo-commentary generated by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) from broadcast footage. These representations are fused and evaluated via a Generative Adversarial Imitation Learning (GAIL) objective, where a discriminator learns to capture the unique mechanical and tactical signatures of elite professionals. By transitioning from generic skill estimation to scouting "by reward," this framework provides a scalable, workflow-aware digital twin system that enables data-driven roster construction and targeted talent discovery across massive candidate pools.

</details>


### [115] [CI-CBM: Class-Incremental Concept Bottleneck Model for Interpretable Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14519)
*Amirhosein Javadi,Tuomas Oikarinen,Tara Javidi,Tsui-Wei Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CI-CBM是一种用于类增量学习的可解释概念瓶颈模型，通过概念正则化和伪概念生成技术，在保持模型可解释性的同时缓解灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题严重，现有方法往往需要在模型可解释性和准确性之间做出妥协，需要一种既能保持可解释性又能维持高性能的方法。

Method: 提出类增量概念瓶颈模型(CI-CBM)，采用概念正则化和伪概念生成技术，在增量学习阶段保持可解释的决策过程。

Result: 在7个数据集上的评估显示，CI-CBM性能与黑盒模型相当，比之前可解释方法平均提升36%准确率，在预训练和非预训练场景下均有效。

Conclusion: CI-CBM证明在增量学习过程中可以保持人类可理解的概念而不牺牲模型性能，提供个体输入的可解释决策和全局决策规则。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a fundamental challenge in continual learning, in which models often forget previous knowledge when fine-tuned on a new task. This issue is especially pronounced in class incremental learning (CIL), which is the most challenging setting in continual learning. Existing methods to address catastrophic forgetting often sacrifice either model interpretability or accuracy. To address this challenge, we introduce ClassIncremental Concept Bottleneck Model (CI-CBM), which leverage effective techniques, including concept regularization and pseudo-concept generation to maintain interpretable decision processes throughout incremental learning phases. Through extensive evaluation on seven datasets, CI-CBM achieves comparable performance to black-box models and outperforms previous interpretable approaches in CIL, with an average 36% accuracy gain. CICBM provides interpretable decisions on individual inputs and understandable global decision rules, as shown in our experiments, thereby demonstrating that human understandable concepts can be maintained during incremental learning without compromising model performance. Our approach is effective in both pretrained and non-pretrained scenarios; in the latter, the backbone is trained from scratch during the first learning phase. Code is publicly available at github.com/importAmir/CI-CBM.

</details>


### [116] [CSRA: Controlled Spectral Residual Augmentation for Robust Sepsis Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14532)
*Honglin Guo,Rihao Chang,He Jiao,Weizhi Nie,Zhongheng Zhang,Yuehao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CSRA：一种用于短窗口ICU时间序列预测的受控谱残差增强框架，通过系统级表示和谱域扰动生成临床合理的轨迹变化，提升脓毒症风险预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脓毒症早期预警需要准确的短期风险预测，但短观察窗口提供有限历史证据，长预测时域减少有效监督样本，传统方法面临数据稀缺挑战。

Method: 提出CSRA框架：1)按临床系统分组变量，提取系统级和全局表示；2)在谱域进行输入自适应残差扰动，生成结构化临床合理轨迹变化；3)端到端训练下游预测器，结合锚点一致性损失和控制器正则化。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV脓毒症队列上，CSRA相比无增强基线在回归任务中MSE降低10.2%、MAE降低3.7%，分类任务也有稳定提升；在更短观察窗口、更长预测时域和小训练数据规模下保持优势，在外部数据集(ZiGongICUinfection)也有效。

Conclusion: CSRA通过受控谱残差增强有效解决了短窗口ICU时间序列预测的数据稀缺问题，具有更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力，适用于临床约束环境下的脓毒症风险预测。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of future risk and disease progression in sepsis is clinically important for early warning and timely intervention in intensive care. However, short-window sepsis prediction remains challenging, because shorter observation windows provide limited historical evidence, whereas longer prediction horizons reduce the number of patient trajectories with valid future supervision. To address this problem, we propose CSRA, a Controlled Spectral Residual Augmentation framework for short-window multi-system ICU time series. CSRA first groups variables by clinical systems and extracts system-level and global representations. It then performs input-adaptive residual perturbation in the spectral domain to generate structured and clinically plausible trajectory variations. To improve augmentation stability and controllability, CSRA is trained end-to-end with the downstream predictor under a unified objective, together with anchor consistency loss and controller regularization. Experiments on a MIMIC-IV sepsis cohort across multiple downstream models show that CSRA is consistently competitive and often superior, reducing regression error by 10.2\% in MSE and 3.7\% in MAE over the non-augmentation baseline, while also yielding consistent gains on classification. CSRA further maintains more favorable performance under shorter observation windows, longer prediction horizons, and smaller training data scales, while also remaining effective on an external clinical dataset~(ZiGongICUinfection), indicating stronger robustness and generalizability in clinically constrained settings.

</details>


### [117] [An unsupervised decision-support framework for multivariate biomarker analysis in athlete monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14534)
*Fernando Barcelos Rosito,Sebastião De Jesus Menezes,Simone Ferreira Sturza,Adriana Seixas,Muriel Figueredo Franco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无监督多变量框架，通过聚类分析识别运动员潜在生理状态，无需损伤标签，区分机械损伤与代谢应激，发现传统单变量监测遗漏的沉默风险表型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动员监测面临小样本、异质生物标志物尺度、重复采样可行性有限、缺乏可靠损伤真实标签等限制，这些因素降低了传统单变量和二元风险模型的解释性和实用性

Method: 提出模块化计算框架，在联合生物标志物空间中操作，整合预处理、临床安全筛查、无监督聚类和基于质心的生理解释。使用业余足球运动员竞争微周期数据学习特征，通过合成数据增强评估鲁棒性和可扩展性。使用Ward层次聚类支持监测和病因学区分，高斯混合模型用于高维设置下的结构稳定性分析

Result: 框架识别出区分机械损伤与代谢应激的连贯特征，同时保持稳态状态。合成数据增强证明了可行性，并检测到通常被单变量监测遗漏的潜在沉默风险表型。结构分析表明在增强和高维设置下具有鲁棒性

Conclusion: 该框架能够从多变量生物标志物数据中无需损伤标签即可解释性地识别潜在生理状态。通过区分机制并揭示传统监测未捕捉的沉默风险模式，为个体化运动员监测和决策制定提供可操作的见解

Abstract: Purpose. Athlete monitoring is constrained by small cohorts, heterogeneous biomarker scales, limited feasibility of repeated sampling, and the lack of reliable injury ground truth. These limitations reduce the interpretability and utility of traditional univariate and binary risk models. This study addresses these challenges by proposing an unsupervised multivariate framework to identify latent physiological states in athletes using real data. Methods. We propose a modular computational framework that operates in the joint biomarker space, integrating preprocessing, clinical safety screening, unsupervised clustering, and centroid-based physiological interpretation. Profiles are learned exclusively from amateur soccer players during a competitive microcycle. Synthetic data augmentation evaluates robustness and scalability. Ward hierarchical clustering supports monitoring and etiological differentiation, while Gaussian Mixture Models (GMM) enable structural stability analysis in high-dimensional settings. Results. The framework identifies coherent profiles that distinguish mechanical damage from metabolic stress while preserving homeostatic states. Synthetic data augmentation demonstrates feasibility and detection of latent silent risk phenotypes typically missed by univariate monitoring. Structural analyses indicate robustness under augmentation and higher-dimensional settings. Conclusion. The framework enables interpretable identification of latent physiological states from multivariate biomarker data without injury labels. By distinguishing mechanisms and revealing silent risk patterns not captured by conventional monitoring, it provides actionable insights for individualized athlete monitoring and decision making.

</details>


### [118] [Predicting Post-Traumatic Epilepsy from Clinical Records using Large Language Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14547)
*Wenhui Cui,Nicholas Swingle,Anand A. Joshi,Dileep Nair,Richard M. Leahy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用大型语言模型编码临床记录，结合梯度提升树分类器，实现创伤后癫痫的早期预测，AUC-ROC达0.892


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创伤后癫痫是创伤性脑损伤后的严重神经系统并发症，早期预测面临临床数据异质性、阳性病例有限、依赖资源密集型神经影像数据等挑战。研究探索是否仅使用常规收集的急性临床记录就能支持早期PTE预测。

Method: 使用TRACK-TBI队列的精选子集，开发自动化PTE预测框架。采用预训练大型语言模型作为固定特征提取器编码临床记录，评估表格特征、LLM生成嵌入和混合特征表示在分层交叉验证下的梯度提升树分类器性能。

Result: LLM嵌入通过捕捉上下文临床信息，相比单独使用表格特征实现了性能提升。最佳性能通过结合表格特征和LLM嵌入的模态感知特征融合策略实现，AUC-ROC为0.892，AUPRC为0.798。急性创伤后癫痫发作、损伤严重程度、神经外科干预和ICU住院是关键预测因素。

Conclusion: 常规急性临床记录包含适合早期PTE风险预测的信息，使用LLM嵌入结合梯度提升树分类器的方法代表了一种有前景的影像预测补充方案。

Abstract: Objective: Post-traumatic epilepsy (PTE) is a debilitating neurological disorder that develops after traumatic brain injury (TBI). Early prediction of PTE remains challenging due to heterogeneous clinical data, limited positive cases, and reliance on resource-intensive neuroimaging data. We investigate whether routinely collected acute clinical records alone can support early PTE prediction using language model-based approaches. Methods: Using a curated subset of the TRACK-TBI cohort, we developed an automated PTE prediction framework that implements pretrained large language models (LLMs) as fixed feature extractors to encode clinical records. Tabular features, LLM-generated embeddings, and hybrid feature representations were evaluated using gradient-boosted tree classifiers under stratified cross-validation. Results: LLM embeddings achieved performance improvements by capturing contextual clinical information compared to using tabular features alone. The best performance was achieved by a modality-aware feature fusion strategy combining tabular features and LLM embeddings, achieving an AUC-ROC of 0.892 and AUPRC of 0.798. Acute post-traumatic seizures, injury severity, neurosurgical intervention, and ICU stay are key contributors to the predictive performance. Significance: These findings demonstrate that routine acute clinical records contain information suitable for early PTE risk prediction using LLM embeddings in conjunction with gradient-boosted tree classifiers. This approach represents a promising complement to imaging-based prediction.

</details>


### [119] [CLion: Efficient Cautious Lion Optimizer with Enhanced Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14587)
*Feihu Huang,Guanyi Zhang,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lion优化器的泛化性能，提出了具有更好泛化能力的CLion优化器，并证明了其理论优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Lion优化器在深度学习训练中表现出色且其收敛性已被研究，但其泛化性能分析仍然缺失。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究Lion优化器的泛化特性，并设计具有更好泛化性能的新优化器。

Method: 1. 使用基于数学归纳法的算法稳定性分析Lion优化器的泛化误差；2. 设计新型CLion优化器，通过谨慎使用符号函数来增强泛化能力；3. 理论分析CLion的收敛性和泛化误差；4. 通过大量数值实验验证CLion的有效性。

Result: 1. 证明了Lion优化器的泛化误差为O(1/(Nτ^T))，其中N为训练样本数，τ为梯度估计器中非零元素的最小绝对值，T为迭代次数；2. 发现SignSGD算法具有与Lion相同的泛化误差；3. CLion优化器具有更低的泛化误差O(1/N)；4. CLion在非凸随机优化中具有O(√d/T^{1/4})的快速收敛率；5. 实验验证了CLion的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次分析了Lion优化器的泛化性能，提出了具有更好泛化能力的CLion优化器。理论证明CLion具有更低的泛化误差和快速的收敛率，实验验证了其有效性。该研究为理解基于符号的优化器的泛化特性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Lion optimizer is a popular learning-based optimization algorithm in machine learning, which shows impressive performance in training many deep learning models. Although convergence property of the Lion optimizer has been studied, its generalization analysis is still missing. To fill this gap, we study generalization property of the Lion via algorithmic stability based on the mathematical induction. Specifically, we prove that the Lion has a generalization error of $O(\frac{1}{Nτ^T})$, where $N$ is training sample size, and $τ>0$ denotes the smallest absolute value of non-zero element in gradient estimator, and $T$ is the total iteration number. In addition, we obtain an interesting byproduct that the SignSGD algorithm has the same generalization error as the Lion. To enhance generalization of the Lion, we design a novel efficient Cautious Lion (i.e., CLion) optimizer by cautiously using sign function. Moreover, we prove that our CLion has a lower generalization error of $O(\frac{1}{N})$ than $O(\frac{1}{Nτ^T})$ of the Lion, since the parameter $τ$ generally is very small. Meanwhile, we study convergence property of our CLion optimizer, and prove that our CLion has a fast convergence rate of $O(\frac{\sqrt{d}}{T^{1/4}})$ under $\ell_1$-norm of gradient for nonconvex stochastic optimization, where $d$ denotes the model dimension.
  Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate effectiveness of our CLion optimizer.

</details>


### [120] [ConfLayers: Adaptive Confidence-based Layer Skipping for Self-Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14612)
*Walaa Amer,Uday das,Fadi Kurdahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConfLayers：一种基于置信度的动态层跳过方法，用于自推测解码中的草稿模型构建，无需训练即可实现1.4倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自推测解码方法中，动态学习层跳过策略的训练开销大且复杂，而启发式方法虽然简单但效果有限，需要一种无需训练、自适应且能提供稳定速度-质量权衡的草稿模型构建方法

Method: 提出ConfLayers框架：迭代计算所有层的置信度分数，基于自适应阈值选择要跳过的层，评估性能，更新最佳选择直到无改进或达到最大迭代次数，形成即插即用的动态草稿模型

Result: 在不同模型和数据集上的评估显示，ConfLayers相比原始LLM生成实现了最高1.4倍的加速，同时保持了输出质量

Conclusion: ConfLayers提供了一种无需训练层跳过策略的动态草稿模型构建方法，避免了训练开销和复杂性，在保持自适应性的同时提供了更一致的加速效果

Abstract: Self-speculative decoding is an inference technique for large language models designed to speed up generation without sacrificing output quality. It combines fast, approximate decoding using a compact version of the model as a draft model with selective re-evaluation by the full target model. Some existing methods form the draft model by dynamically learning which layers to skip during inference, effectively creating a smaller subnetwork to speed up computation. However, using heuristic-based approaches to select layers to skip can often be simpler and more effective. In this paper, we propose ConfLayers, a dynamic plug-and-play approach to forming the draft model in self-speculative decoding via confidence-based intermediate layer skipping. The process iteratively computes confidence scores for all layers, selects layers to skip based on an adaptive threshold, evaluates the performance of the resulting set, and updates the best selection until no further improvement is achieved or a maximum number of iterations is reached. This framework avoids the overhead and complexity of training a layer skipping policy and can provide more consistent speed-quality trade-offs while preserving the adaptivity of the draft model to diverse tasks and datasets. The performance evaluation of ConfLayers across different models and datasets shows that our novel approach offers up to 1.4x speedup over vanilla LLM generation.

</details>


### [121] [ELMoE-3D: Leveraging Intrinsic Elasticity of MoE for Hybrid-Bonding-Enabled Self-Speculative Decoding in On-Premises Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14626)
*Yuseon Choi,Jingu Lee,Jungjun Oh,Sunjoo Whang,Byeongcheol Kim,Minsung Kim,Hoi-Jun Yoo,Sangjin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELMoE-3D：基于混合键合技术的硬件-软件协同设计框架，通过弹性自推测解码和位嵌套执行，在3D堆叠硬件上实现MoE模型高效推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型推理面临内存瓶颈问题：内存中心架构计算利用率低，推测解码在MoE中因需要加载专家而效果受限，尤其是在小批量情况下

Method: 提出ELMoE-3D框架，包含弹性自推测解码（结合专家缓存和自草稿模型）和LSB增强的位切片架构，利用混合键合高带宽和3D堆叠硬件

Result: 在批量大小1-16范围内，相比朴素MoE推理平均获得6.6倍加速和4.4倍能效提升；相比最佳现有加速器基线获得2.2倍加速和1.4倍能效提升

Conclusion: ELMoE-3D通过硬件-软件协同设计，利用MoE的专家和位弹性特性，有效解决了MoE推理的内存瓶颈问题，实现了跨批量大小的高效推理

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have become the dominant architecture for large-scale language models, yet on-premises serving remains fundamentally memory-bound as batching turns sparse per-token compute into dense memory activation. Memory-centric architectures (PIM, NMP) improve bandwidth but leave compute underutilized under MoE's low arithmetic intensity at high batch sizes. Speculative decoding (SD) trades idle compute for fewer target invocations, yet verification must load experts even for rejected tokens, severely limiting its benefit in MoE especially at low batch sizes. We propose ELMoE-3D, a hybrid-bonding (HB)-based HW-SW co-designed framework that unifies cache-based acceleration and speculative decoding to offer overall speedup across batch sizes. We identify two intrinsic elasticity axes of MoE-expert and bit-and jointly scale them to construct Elastic Self-Speculative Decoding (Elastic-SD), which serves as both an expert cache and a strongly aligned self-draft model accelerated by high HB bandwidth. Our LSB-augmented bit-sliced architecture exploits inherent redundancy in bit-slice representations to natively support bit-nested execution. On our 3D-stacked hardware, ELMoE-3D achieves an average $6.6\times$ speedup and $4.4\times$ energy efficiency gain over naive MoE serving on xPU across batch sizes 1-16, and delivers $2.2\times$ speedup and $1.4\times$ energy efficiency gain over the best-performing prior accelerator baseline.

</details>


### [122] [Mean Flow Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14698)
*Xiaoyi Dong,Xi Sheryl Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MeanFlow模型作为扩散模型的替代方案，用于强化学习策略表示，显著提升训练和推理效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型作为强化学习策略表示虽然表达能力强，但其迭代生成过程带来了显著的训练和推理开销，需要更高效的替代方案

Method: 使用MeanFlow模型（少步流式生成模型）表示策略，在最大熵强化学习框架下通过软策略迭代优化，解决动作似然评估和软策略改进两个关键挑战

Result: 在MuJoCo和DeepMind Control Suite基准测试中，Mean Flow Policy Optimization (MFPO)方法性能达到或超过当前扩散基线，同时显著减少训练和推理时间

Conclusion: MeanFlow模型是扩散模型的有效替代方案，在保持性能的同时大幅提升强化学习策略的训练和推理效率

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently emerged as expressive policy representations for online reinforcement learning (RL). However, their iterative generative processes introduce substantial training and inference overhead. To overcome this limitation, we propose to represent policies using MeanFlow models, a class of few-step flow-based generative models, to improve training and inference efficiency over diffusion-based RL approaches. To promote exploration, we optimize MeanFlow policies under the maximum entropy RL framework via soft policy iteration, and address two key challenges specific to MeanFlow policies: action likelihood evaluation and soft policy improvement. Experiments on MuJoCo and DeepMind Control Suite benchmarks demonstrate that our method, Mean Flow Policy Optimization (MFPO), achieves performance comparable to or exceeding current diffusion-based baselines while considerably reducing training and inference time. Our code is available at https://github.com/MFPolicy/MFPO.

</details>


### [123] [A Mechanistic Account of Attention Sinks in GPT-2: One Circuit, Broader Implications for Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14722)
*Yuval Ran-Milo,Hila Ofek,Shahar Mendel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现Transformer中的注意力下沉现象（attention sink）源于学习到的查询偏置、第一层MLP对位置编码的变换以及键投影结构的相互作用，这些组件各自独立可移除，表明不同架构可能通过不同电路产生注意力下沉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Transformer模型中常见的注意力下沉现象（对第一个位置注意力异常高），旨在理解其产生机制，为缓解该现象提供理论基础，并推动更广泛的注意力模式研究。

Method: 采用结构分析与因果干预相结合的方法，在GPT-2风格模型上研究注意力下沉现象，验证范围涵盖自然语言、数学和代码输入，分析学习到的查询偏置、第一层MLP对位置编码的变换以及键投影结构之间的相互作用。

Result: 发现注意力下沉由三个组件的相互作用引起：学习到的查询偏置、第一层MLP对位置编码的变换以及键投影结构。关键发现是每个组件都可独立移除而不影响下沉现象的出现，表明不同架构可能通过不同电路产生注意力下沉。

Conclusion: 注意力下沉现象可通过多种不同电路实现，这一发现为缓解注意力下沉提供了理论指导，并强调了需要更广泛研究注意力下沉现象为何普遍存在。

Abstract: Transformers commonly exhibit an attention sink: disproportionately high attention to the first position. We study this behavior in GPT-2-style models with learned query biases and absolute positional embeddings. Combining structural analysis with causal interventions, validated across natural-language, mathematical, and code inputs, we find that the sink arises from the interaction among (i) a learned query bias, (ii) the first-layer MLP transformation of the positional encoding, and (iii) structure in the key projection. Crucially, each component we identify is individually dispensable: architectures omitting each of them robustly exhibit sinks. This indicates that attention sinks may arise through distinct circuits across architectures. These findings inform mitigation of sinks, and motivate broader investigation into why sinks emerge.

</details>


### [124] [Expressivity of Transformers: A Tropical Geometry Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14727)
*Ye Su,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文引入热带几何框架量化Transformer的几何表达能力，证明自注意力在零温度极限下等价于Power Voronoi图，多头自注意力通过Minkowski和实现复杂度爆炸，并推导出Transformer线性区域数量的紧渐近界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化Transformer的几何表达能力，理解其空间划分能力的内在机制，特别是多头自注意力的组合优势和深度架构的复杂度增长规律。

Method: 引入热带几何框架，将自注意力建模为向量值热带有理映射，证明其在零温度极限下等价于Power Voronoi图；通过Minkowski和牛顿多面体分析多头注意力；推导深度Transformer线性区域数量的渐近界。

Result: 证明自注意力精确计算为Power Voronoi图；多头注意力通过Minkowski和实现复杂度从O(N)扩展到O(N^H)；推导出深度Transformer线性区域数量为Θ(N^{d_model L})；证明有限温度软注意力通过指数紧微分近似保持拓扑划分。

Conclusion: Transformer具有强大的几何表达能力，其空间划分能力可通过热带几何精确刻画；多头机制和深度架构驱动组合复杂度爆炸；这种理想化多面体骨架在有限温度下保持几何稳定性。

Abstract: To quantify the geometric expressivity of transformers, we introduce a tropical geometry framework to characterize their exact spatial partitioning capabilities. By modeling self-attention as a vector-valued tropical rational map, we prove it evaluates exactly to a Power Voronoi Diagram in the zero-temperature limit. Building on this equivalence, we establish a combinatorial rationale for Multi-Head Self-Attention (MHSA): via the Minkowski sum of Newton polytopes, multi-head aggregation expands the polyhedral complexity to $\mathcal{O}(N^H)$, overcoming the $\mathcal{O}(N)$ bottleneck of single heads. Extending this to deep architectures, we derive the first tight asymptotic bounds on the number of linear regions in transformers ($Θ(N^{d_{\text{model}}L})$), demonstrating a combinatorial explosion driven intrinsically by sequence length $N$, ambient embedding dimension $d_{\text{model}}$, and network depth $L$. Importantly, we guarantee that this idealized polyhedral skeleton is geometrically stable: finite-temperature soft attention preserves these topological partitions via exponentially tight differential approximation bounds.

</details>


### [125] [Constraint-based Pre-training: From Structured Constraints to Scalable Model Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14769)
*Fu Feng,Yucheng Xie,Ruixiao Shi,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于约束的预训练范式WeiT，通过结构化约束将尺寸无关知识解耦到可重用权重模板中，将尺寸特定适配分配给轻量级权重缩放器，实现可变尺寸模型初始化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预训练产生固定规模模型，而实际部署需要不同尺寸模型，当目标模型规模与预训练规模不同时存在局限性

Method: 提出约束基预训练范式，使用Kronecker基约束正则化预训练过程；模型参数表示为权重模板的拼接和加权聚合，由轻量级权重缩放器控制自适应连接

Result: 在Image Classification、Image Generation和Embodied Control等感知和具身学习任务中，WeiT在初始化不同深度和宽度模型时达到SOTA性能，支持Transformer和Convolution架构，实现更快收敛和更好性能

Conclusion: WeiT通过约束基预训练范式有效解决可变尺寸模型初始化问题，将尺寸无关知识解耦到可重用模板中，实现灵活高效的下游模型构建

Abstract: The pre-training and fine-tuning paradigm has become the dominant approach for model adaptation. However, conventional pre-training typically yields models at a fixed scale, whereas practical deployment often requires models of varying sizes, exposing its limitations when target model scales differ from those used during pre-training. To address this, we propose an innovative constraint-based pre-training paradigm that imposes structured constraints during pre-training to disentangle size-agnostic knowledge into reusable weight templates, while assigning size-specific adaptation to lightweight weight scalers, thereby reformulating variable-sized model initialization as a multi-task adaptation problem. Within this paradigm, we further introduce WeiT, which employs Kronecker-based constraints to regularize the pre-training process. Specifically, model parameters are represented as compositions of weight templates via concatenation and weighted aggregation, with adaptive connections governed by lightweight weight scalers whose parameters are learned from limited data. This design enables flexible and efficient construction of model weights across diverse downstream scales. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficiency and effectiveness of WeiT, achieving state-of-the-art performance in initializing models with varying depths and widths across a broad range of perception and embodied learning tasks, including Image Classification, Image Generation, and Embodied Control. Moreover, its effectiveness generalizes to both Transformer-based and Convolution-based architectures, consistently enabling faster convergence and improved performance even under full training.

</details>


### [126] [xFODE+: Explainable Type-2 Fuzzy Additive ODEs for Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14880)
*Ertugrul Kececi,Tufan Kumbasar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: xFODE+是一种可解释的Type-2模糊加性ODE模型，用于系统辨识中的不确定性量化，在保持预测精度的同时提供预测区间和物理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习推动了数据驱动的系统辨识，但可靠使用需要不确定性量化。现有模糊ODE模型能产生预测区间但可解释性有限，需要同时具备准确预测、不确定性量化和可解释性的模型。

Method: 提出xFODE+模型，使用区间Type-2模糊逻辑系统实现模糊加性模型，约束隶属函数仅激活两个相邻规则以减少重叠，保持局部透明推理。通过类型归约集聚合构建状态更新和预测区间，在深度学习框架中使用复合损失函数联合优化预测精度和预测区间质量。

Result: 在基准系统辨识数据集上，xFODE+在预测区间质量上与FODE相当，预测精度可比，同时提供了可解释性。

Conclusion: xFODE+成功地将不确定性量化、预测准确性和可解释性结合在一个统一的系统辨识框架中，为可靠的数据驱动建模提供了新方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in Deep Learning (DL) have boosted data-driven System Identification (SysID), but reliable use requires Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) alongside accurate predictions. Although UQ-capable models such as Fuzzy ODE (FODE) can produce Prediction Intervals (PIs), they offer limited interpretability. We introduce Explainable Type-2 Fuzzy Additive ODEs for UQ (xFODE+), an interpretable SysID model which produces PIs alongside point predictions while retaining physically meaningful incremental states. xFODE+ implements each fuzzy additive model with Interval Type-2 Fuzzy Logic Systems (IT2-FLSs) and constraints membership functions to the activation of two neighboring rules, limiting overlap and keeping inference locally transparent. The type-reduced sets produced by the IT2-FLSs are aggregated to construct the state update together with the PIs. The model is trained in a DL framework via a composite loss that jointly optimizes prediction accuracy and PI quality. Results on benchmark SysID datasets show that xFODE+ matches FODE in PI quality and achieves comparable accuracy, while providing interpretability.

</details>


### [127] [xFODE: An Explainable Fuzzy Additive ODE Framework for System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14883)
*Ertugrul Kececi,Tufan Kumbasar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可解释模糊常微分方程（xFODE）框架，通过增量形式状态定义和模糊加性模型增强系统辨识的物理可解释性，同时保持与深度学习方法相当的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的系统辨识方法（如NODE/FODE）存在两个主要问题：1）重构的系统状态缺乏明确的物理意义；2）输入对状态导数的贡献难以解释。需要开发既能保持高精度又能提供可解释性的系统辨识框架。

Method: 提出xFODE框架：1）采用增量形式定义状态，赋予物理意义；2）使用模糊加性模型近似状态导数，增强每个输入的可解释性；3）开发分区策略（PSs），通过训练时结构化前件空间，确保任意输入只激活两个连续规则，降低局部推理复杂度并增强前件空间可解释性；4）构建支持端到端优化的深度学习框架，包含参数化隶属函数学习。

Result: 在基准系统辨识数据集上，xFODE能够匹配NODE、FODE和NLARX模型的准确性，同时提供可解释的洞察。框架在保持高精度的前提下，显著提升了模型的可解释性。

Conclusion: xFODE成功解决了传统深度学习系统辨识方法缺乏可解释性的问题，通过增量状态定义、模糊加性模型和分区策略的组合，实现了高精度与可解释性的平衡，为复杂非线性动态系统的建模提供了新的可解释框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in Deep Learning (DL) have strengthened data-driven System Identification (SysID), with Neural and Fuzzy Ordinary Differential Equation (NODE/FODE) models achieving high accuracy in nonlinear dynamic modeling. Yet, system states in these frameworks are often reconstructed without clear physical meaning, and input contributions to the state derivatives remain difficult to interpret. To address these limitations, we propose Explainable FODE (xFODE), an interpretable SysID framework with integrated DL-based training. In xFODE, we define states in an incremental form to provide them with physical meanings. We employ fuzzy additive models to approximate the state derivative, thereby enhancing interpretability per input. To provide further interpretability, Partitioning Strategies (PSs) are developed, enabling the training of fuzzy additive models with explainability. By structuring the antecedent space during training so that only two consecutive rules are activated for any given input, PSs not only yield lower complexity for local inference but also enhance the interpretability of the antecedent space. To train xFODE, we present a DL framework with parameterized membership function learning that supports end-to-end optimization. Across benchmark SysID datasets, xFODE matches the accuracy of NODE, FODE, and NLARX models while providing interpretable insights.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Importance Sampling: Rejection-Gated Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14895)
*Ziwu Sun,Zhen Gao,Jiyong Zhang,Jiaheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RGPO提出了一种新的策略优化视角：通过可微的接受门函数选择可信样本驱动策略更新，替代传统重要性采样重加权方法，在保证梯度方差有限的同时控制偏差，实现高效稳定的强化学习优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统策略优化方法（如重要性采样）在处理重尾分布的重要性采样比时存在梯度方差发散问题，而现有拒绝采样方法通常作为数据层面的启发式方法，缺乏与优化过程的直接集成。需要一种既能控制梯度方差又能保持优化稳定性的新方法。

Method: RGPO使用平滑可微的接受门函数α_θ(s,a)=g(r_θ(s,a))替代重要性采样比，该门函数直接参与梯度计算并与策略同步更新。该方法将拒绝提升为优化原则，提供了统一框架：TRPO、PPO和REINFORCE的梯度都对应特定的有效梯度权重w(r)=g'(r)*r。在RLHF应用中采用双比率门，将学习锚定到先前策略和参考模型。

Result: RGPO保证了即使在重要性采样比呈重尾分布时梯度方差也有限且有限，同时仅引入有界可控的偏差，并提供类似TRPO的近似单调策略改进保证。在Anthropic HH-RLHF数据集上对Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct进行在线偏好微调时，RGPO实现了帕累托最优：在线RL方法中奖励最高（比PPO-RLHF高14.8%），与参考模型的KL散度最低（比PPO-RLHF低16.0%，比GRPO低53.1%）。

Conclusion: RGPO提供了一种新颖的策略优化框架，通过将拒绝采样提升为优化原则，在计算成本与PPO相当、无需二阶优化的情况下，实现了梯度方差控制与优化稳定性的平衡，特别适用于RLHF风格的偏好对齐任务，展现出优越的性能表现。

Abstract: We propose a new perspective on policy optimization: rather than reweighting all samples by their importance ratios, an optimizer should select which samples are trustworthy enough to drive a policy update. Building on this view, we introduce Rejection-Gated Policy Optimization (RGPO), which replaces the importance sampling ratio r_theta = pi_theta / pi_old with a smooth, differentiable acceptance gate alpha_theta(s, a) = g(r_theta(s, a)) in the range [0, 1]. Unlike prior work that applies rejection sampling as a data-level heuristic before training, RGPO elevates rejection to an optimization principle: the gate participates directly in gradient computation and is implicitly updated alongside the policy. RGPO provides a unified framework: the policy gradients of TRPO, PPO, and REINFORCE all correspond to specific choices of the effective gradient weight w(r) = g'(r) * r. We prove that RGPO guarantees finite, bounded gradient variance even when importance sampling ratios are heavy-tailed (where IS variance diverges). We further show that RGPO incurs only a bounded, controllable bias and provides an approximate monotonic policy improvement guarantee analogous to TRPO. RGPO matches PPO in computational cost, requires no second-order optimization, and extends naturally to RLHF-style preference alignment. In online preference fine-tuning of Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct on Anthropic HH-RLHF (n = 3 seeds), RGPO uses a dual-ratio gate that anchors learning to both the previous policy and the reference model, achieving a Pareto-dominant outcome: the highest reward among online RL methods (+14.8% vs. PPO-RLHF) and the lowest KL divergence to the reference model (-16.0% vs. PPO-RLHF, -53.1% vs. GRPO).

</details>


### [129] [Multi-User mmWave Beam and Rate Adaptation via Combinatorial Satisficing Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14908)
*Emre Özyıldırım,Barış Yaycı,Umut Eren Akturk,Cem Tekin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAT-CTS策略用于毫米波MISO系统的波束和速率自适应，引入满意吞吐量阈值τ_r，将联合优化建模为组合半赌博机问题，在有限时间内实现接近目标性能的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多用户毫米波MISO系统中，基站使用有限码本的模拟波束成形，用户设备为单天线，需要基于ACK/NACK反馈学习传输成功率。传统方法追求最大化吞吐量，但实际应用中用户有服务质量(QoS)要求，需要满足特定吞吐量阈值而非单纯最大化。

Method: 将联合波束和速率自适应建模为组合半赌博机问题，提出SAT-CTS策略：结合保守置信度估计和后验采样，引导学习过程满足满意吞吐量阈值τ_r而非单纯最大化。策略包含两个阶段：当τ_r可实现时，策略收敛到目标；当τ_r不可实现时，策略在有限期望瞬态后重启CTS轮次。

Result: 理论贡献：首次为具有满意目标的组合半赌博机提供有限时间遗憾界。当τ_r可实现时，累积满意遗憾有与时间无关的常数上界；当τ_r不可实现时，SAT-CTS仅产生有限期望瞬态，之后遗憾由重启CTS轮次的遗憾贡献之和决定，获得O((log T)²)标准遗憾界。实验验证：在时变稀疏多径信道中，SAT-CTS持续减少满意遗憾，保持竞争力的标准遗憾，同时实现有利的平均吞吐量和用户间公平性。

Conclusion: SAT-CTS策略能够在无需信道状态知识的情况下，通过反馈高效学习，公平分配波束和速率以满足QoS目标。该方法将学习目标从最大化转向满意化，更符合实际通信系统的服务质量要求，为毫米波系统中的资源分配提供了新的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: We study downlink beam and rate adaptation in a multi-user mmWave MISO system where multiple base stations (BSs), each using analog beamforming from finite codebooks, serve multiple single-antenna user equipments (UEs) with a unique beam per UE and discrete data transmission rates. BSs learn about transmission success based on ACK/NACK feedback. To encode service goals, we introduce a satisficing throughput threshold $τ_r$ and cast joint beam and rate adaptation as a combinatorial semi-bandit over beam-rate tuples. Within this framework, we propose SAT-CTS, a lightweight, threshold-aware policy that blends conservative confidence estimates with posterior sampling, steering learning toward meeting $τ_r$ rather than merely maximizing. Our main theoretical contribution provides the first finite-time regret bounds for combinatorial semi-bandits with satisficing objective: when $τ_r$ is realizable, we upper bound the cumulative satisficing regret to the target with a time-independent constant, and when $τ_r$ is non-realizable, we show that SAT-CTS incurs only a finite expected transient outside committed CTS rounds, after which its regret is governed by the sum of the regret contributions of restarted CTS rounds, yielding an $O((\log T)^2)$ standard regret bound. On the practical side, we evaluate the performance via cumulative satisficing regret to $τ_r$ alongside standard regret and fairness. Experiments with time-varying sparse multipath channels show that SAT-CTS consistently reduces satisficing regret and maintains competitive standard regret, while achieving favorable average throughput and fairness across users, indicating that feedback-efficient learning can equitably allocate beams and rates to meet QoS targets without channel state knowledge.

</details>


### [130] [LongAct: Harnessing Intrinsic Activation Patterns for Long-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14922)
*Bowen Ping,Zijun Chen,Tingfeng Hui,Qize Yu,Chenxuan Li,Junchi Yan,Baobao Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongAct：一种基于显著性引导的稀疏权重更新策略，通过选择性更新与高幅度激活相关的权重来提升大语言模型的长上下文推理能力，在LongBench v2上获得约8%的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法主要关注奖励工程或数据合成，很少利用模型内在表征特性来指导训练过程。作者观察到在处理长上下文时，查询和关键向量中存在高幅度激活，这些激活在模型量化中被证明是关键特征，而长上下文推理本身具有稀疏结构，因此假设这些权重是有效模型优化的关键驱动因素。

Method: 提出LongAct策略，从均匀更新转向显著性引导的稀疏更新。该方法基于模型在处理长上下文时产生的高幅度激活，选择性更新仅与这些显著激活相关的权重，而不是更新所有权重。这种方法利用了长上下文推理的稀疏性特征。

Result: 在LongBench v2基准测试上获得约8%的性能提升，在RULER基准测试上展现出增强的泛化能力。方法具有显著通用性，在GRPO和DAPO等多种强化学习算法上都能一致提升性能。消融研究表明关注这些显著特征是解锁长上下文潜力的关键。

Conclusion: 通过利用模型内在表征特性，特别是高幅度激活的显著性特征，LongAct策略能够有效提升大语言模型的长上下文推理能力。该方法为强化学习训练提供了新的视角，即通过稀疏、显著性引导的权重更新来优化模型性能，而不是传统的均匀更新策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a critical driver for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). While recent advancements have focused on reward engineering or data synthesis, few studies exploit the model's intrinsic representation characteristics to guide the training process. In this paper, we first observe the presence of high-magnitude activations within the query and key vectors when processing long contexts. Drawing inspiration from model quantization -- which establishes the criticality of such high-magnitude activations -- and the insight that long-context reasoning inherently exhibits a sparse structure, we hypothesize that these weights serve as the pivotal drivers for effective model optimization. Based on this insight, we propose LongAct, a strategy that shifts from uniform to saliency-guided sparse updates. By selectively updating only the weights associated with these significant activations, LongAct achieves an approximate 8% improvement on LongBench v2 and enhances generalization on the RULER benchmark. Furthermore, our method exhibits remarkable universality, consistently boosting performance across diverse RL algorithms such as GRPO and DAPO. Extensive ablation studies suggest that focusing on these salient features is key to unlocking long-context potential.

</details>


### [131] [Improving Sparse Autoencoder with Dynamic Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14925)
*Dongsheng Wang,Jinsen Zhang,Dawei Su,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于sparsemax动态稀疏注意力机制的新型稀疏自编码器，能够根据神经元复杂度自动推断激活数量，在保持良好重构的同时提高可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器在解释基础模型激活方面有潜力，但确定每个神经元的最佳稀疏度具有挑战性：过度稀疏会导致重构效果差，而稀疏不足会损害可解释性。现有激活函数需要额外的稀疏正则化或精心选择的超参数。

Method: 提出基于交叉注意力架构的新型SAE，将潜在特征作为查询，可学习字典作为键值矩阵。采用sparsemax注意力策略，根据每个神经元的复杂度自动推断稀疏元素集合，实现数据依赖的激活数量确定。

Result: 通过综合评估和可视化，该方法成功实现了更低的重构损失，同时产生高质量的概念表示，特别是在top-n分类任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: 基于sparsemax的动态稀疏注意力机制能够有效平衡重构质量和可解释性之间的权衡，为稀疏自编码器提供了一种更灵活、通用的激活函数方案。

Abstract: Recently, sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a promising technique for interpreting activations in foundation models by disentangling features into a sparse set of concepts. However, identifying the optimal level of sparsity for each neuron remains challenging in practice: excessive sparsity can lead to poor reconstruction, whereas insufficient sparsity may harm interpretability. While existing activation functions such as ReLU and TopK provide certain sparsity guarantees, they typically require additional sparsity regularization or cherry-picked hyperparameters. We show in this paper that dynamically sparse attention mechanisms using sparsemax can bridge this trade-off, due to their ability to determine the activation numbers in a data-dependent manner. Specifically, we first explore a new class of SAEs based on the cross-attention architecture with the latent features as queries and the learnable dictionary as the key and value matrices. To encourage sparse pattern learning, we employ a sparsemax-based attention strategy that automatically infers a sparse set of elements according to the complexity of each neuron, resulting in a more flexible and general activation function. Through comprehensive evaluation and visualization, we show that our approach successfully achieves lower reconstruction loss while producing high-quality concepts, particularly in top-n classification tasks.

</details>


### [132] [Calibration-Gated LLM Pseudo-Observations for Online Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14961)
*Maksim Pershin,Ivan Golovanov,Pavel Baltabaev,Natalia Trankova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出在Disjoint LinUCB算法中注入LLM伪观测值来缓解冷启动问题，通过校准门控衰减调度控制LLM预测的权重，实验显示任务特定提示能减少遗憾但通用提示会增加遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文多臂赌博机算法在冷启动阶段面临高遗憾问题，因为学习者缺乏足够数据区分好坏臂。需要利用LLM的先验知识来加速学习过程。

Method: 在Disjoint LinUCB中注入LLM伪观测值：每轮结束后，LLM预测未播放臂的反事实奖励，作为加权伪观测值注入学习器。通过校准门控衰减调度控制权重，使用指数移动平均跟踪LLM在已播放臂上的预测准确性。

Result: 在UCI Mushroom（2臂，非对称奖励）和MIND-small（5臂新闻推荐）环境中评估。任务特定提示使LLM伪观测值在MIND上相对纯LinUCB减少19%累积遗憾。但通用反事实提示框架在两个环境中都增加遗憾，表明提示设计是主导因素。

Conclusion: LLM伪观测值能有效缓解冷启动问题，但提示设计比衰减调度或校准门控参数选择更重要。分析了校准门控在小预测误差领域的失败模式，并为伪观测值权重的偏差-方差权衡提供了理论动机。

Abstract: Contextual bandit algorithms suffer from high regret during cold-start, when the learner has insufficient data to distinguish good arms from bad. We propose augmenting Disjoint LinUCB with LLM pseudo-observations: after each round, a large language model predicts counterfactual rewards for the unplayed arms, and these predictions are injected into the learner as weighted pseudo-observations. The injection weight is controlled by a calibration-gated decay schedule that tracks the LLM's prediction accuracy on played arms via an exponential moving average; high calibration error suppresses the LLM's influence, while accurate predictions receive higher weight during the critical early rounds. We evaluate on two contextual bandit environments - UCI Mushroom (2-arm, asymmetric rewards) and MIND-small (5-arm news recommendation) - and find that when equipped with a task-specific prompt, LLM pseudo-observations reduce cumulative regret by 19% on MIND relative to pure LinUCB. However, generic counterfactual prompt framing increases regret on both environments, demonstrating that prompt design is the dominant factor, more important than the choice of decay schedule or calibration gating parameters. We analyze the failure modes of calibration gating on domains with small prediction errors and provide a theoretical motivation for the bias-variance trade-off governing pseudo-observation weight.

</details>


### [133] [Blazing the trails before beating the path: Sample-efficient Monte-Carlo planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14974)
*Jean-Bastien Grill,Michal Valko,Rémi Munos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TrailBlazer是一种用于MDP中蒙特卡洛规划的高效算法，通过仅探索接近最优策略可达的状态子集来利用MDP结构，提供依赖于接近最优状态数量的样本复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有有限或无限状态转移的MDP中进行高效规划，需要利用MDP结构特性，仅探索接近最优策略可达的状态子集，避免指数级运行时间，同时提供理论保证。

Method: TrailBlazer算法扩展了蒙特卡洛采样方法，交替进行动作的最大化和下一状态的期望计算，通过智能探索策略实现计算效率，避免StOP算法的指数复杂度。

Result: 算法提供了样本复杂度保证，该保证依赖于接近最优状态数量的度量，实现了比传统方法更高的样本效率，同时保持简单实现和计算效率。

Conclusion: TrailBlazer为MDP中的蒙特卡洛规划提供了一种高效解决方案，平衡了样本效率、计算复杂度和理论保证，适用于需要快速决策的实际机器人应用场景。

Abstract: You are a robot and you live in a Markov decision process (MDP) with a finite or an infinite number of transitions from state-action to next states. You got brains and so you plan before you act. Luckily, your roboparents equipped you with a generative model to do some Monte-Carlo planning. The world is waiting for you and you have no time to waste. You want your planning to be efficient. Sample-efficient. Indeed, you want to exploit the possible structure of the MDP by exploring only a subset of states reachable by following near-optimal policies. You want guarantees on sample complexity that depend on a measure of the quantity of near-optimal states. You want something, that is an extension of Monte-Carlo sampling (for estimating an expectation) to problems that alternate maximization (over actions) and expectation (over next states). But you do not want to StOP with exponential running time, you want something simple to implement and computationally efficient. You want it all and you want it now. You want TrailBlazer.

</details>


### [134] [DLink: Distilling Layer-wise and Dominant Knowledge from EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15016)
*Jingyuan Wang,Meiyan Xu,Zhihao Jia,Chenyu Liu,Xinliang Zhou,Ziyu Jia,Yong Li,Fang Li,Junfeng Yao,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DLink是一个用于脑电图基础模型知识蒸馏的统一框架，通过动态路由、模仿-压缩管道和频谱蒸馏，将大型模型压缩为紧凑学生模型，在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算和内存成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电图基础模型虽然具有强大的跨被试和跨任务泛化能力，但计算和内存成本过高，难以部署在嵌入式脑机接口系统上。传统知识蒸馏方法对脑电图基础模型效果不佳，因为任务相关语义分布在中间层，且激进的维度压缩会扭曲振荡结构。

Method: 提出DLink框架，包含三个关键创新：1）动态路由器自适应聚合教师层以捕获主导中间表示；2）EEG MiC学生采用模仿-压缩管道，先继承高维教师特征，再应用结构化时空压缩以避免沉重的分类头；3）频谱蒸馏在频域对齐师生表示，以正则化压缩并减轻混叠和时间抖动。

Result: 在四个脑电图基准测试中，DLink使紧凑学生模型优于轻量级基线，同时以显著更低的模型大小和推理成本接近完全微调的基础模型性能。

Conclusion: DLink成功解决了脑电图基础模型知识蒸馏的挑战，实现了高效的模型压缩，为嵌入式脑机接口系统的实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: EEG foundation models (FMs) achieve strong cross-subject and cross-task generalization but impose substantial computational and memory costs that hinder deployment on embedded BCI systems. Knowledge distillation is a natural solution; however, conventional methods fail for EEG FMs because task-relevant semantics are often distributed across intermediate layers, and aggressive dimensionality reduction can distort oscillatory structure via representational collapse and aliasing. To address these challenges, we propose DLink (Distilling Layer-wise and Dominant Knowledge), a unified framework for transferring knowledge from large EEG FMs to compact students with three key innovations: (1) a dynamic Router that adaptively aggregates teacher layers to capture dominant intermediate representations; (2) an EEG MiC student with a Mimic-then-Compress pipeline, which inherits high-dimensional teacher features and then applies structured spatio-temporal compression to avoid a heavy classification head; and (3) spectral distillation that aligns teacher-student representations in the frequency domain to regularize compression and mitigate aliasing and temporal jitter. Experiments on four EEG benchmarks show that DLink enables compact students to outperform lightweight baselines while approaching fully fine-tuned FM performance at substantially lower model size and inference cost.

</details>


### [135] [When Fairness Metrics Disagree: Evaluating the Reliability of Demographic Fairness Assessment in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15038)
*Khalid Adnan Alsayed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现公平性评估存在不一致性问题，不同公平性指标对同一机器学习系统可能得出矛盾结论，提出了公平性分歧指数来量化这种不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习公平性评估通常依赖少量指标，隐含假设这些指标能提供一致可靠的结论，但不同指标可能产生冲突评估，需要系统性研究这种不一致性问题。

Method: 以人脸识别为实验场景，在多个群体划分下使用多种常用公平性指标评估模型性能，包括错误率差异和基于性能的度量，并提出了公平性分歧指数来量化指标间的不一致性。

Result: 公平性评估结果显著依赖于所选指标，导致关于模型偏见的矛盾结论；公平性分歧指数显示指标间不一致性高，且在不同阈值和模型配置下保持高水平。

Conclusion: 当前公平性评估实践存在关键局限性，单一指标报告不足以进行可靠的偏见评估，需要多指标综合分析。

Abstract: The evaluation of fairness in machine learning systems has become a central concern in high-stakes applications, including biometric recognition, healthcare decision-making, and automated risk assessment. Existing approaches typically rely on a small number of fairness metrics to assess model behaviour across group partitions, implicitly assuming that these metrics provide consistent and reliable conclusions. However, different fairness metrics capture distinct statistical properties of model performance and may therefore produce conflicting assessments when applied to the same system. In this work, we investigate the consistency of fairness evaluation by conducting a systematic multi-metric analysis of demographic bias in machine learning models. Using face recognition as a controlled experimental setting, we evaluate model performance across multiple group partitions under a range of commonly used fairness metrics, including error-rate disparities and performance-based measures. Our results demonstrate that fairness assessments can vary significantly depending on the choice of metrics, leading to contradictory conclusions regarding model bias. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the Fairness Disagreement Index (FDI), a measure designed to capture the degree of inconsistency across fairness metrics. We further show that disagreement remains high across thresholds and model configurations. These findings highlight a critical limitation in current fairness evaluation practices and suggest that single-metric reporting is insufficient for reliable bias assessment.

</details>


### [136] [No More Guessing: a Verifiable Gradient Inversion Attack in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15063)
*Francesco Diana,Chuan Xu,André Nusser,Giovanni Neglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可验证梯度反演攻击(VGIA)，通过代数验证测试为重构样本提供正确性证明，挑战表格数据不易受攻击的认知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度反演攻击在联邦学习中难以解耦多记录贡献，缺乏内在验证机制；表格数据因缺乏人工可解释性被认为更安全，但这种认知需要挑战

Method: 采用ReLU泄漏的几何视角：全连接层激活边界定义输入空间超平面；引入基于子空间的代数验证测试，检测超平面限定区域是否恰好包含一个记录；认证隔离后解析恢复特征向量，通过轻量优化步骤重构目标

Result: 在大量批次规模的表格基准测试中实现精确记录和目标恢复，在现有最先进攻击失败或无法评估重构保真度的场景中表现优异；相比先前几何方法，更有效分配超平面查询，实现更少攻击轮次的快速重构

Conclusion: 表格数据同样易受梯度反演攻击，VGIA通过可验证正确性证明挑战了表格数据更安全的认知，为联邦学习隐私保护提供重要启示

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks threaten client privacy in federated learning by reconstructing training samples from clients' shared gradients. Gradients aggregate contributions from multiple records and existing attacks may fail to disentangle them, yielding incorrect reconstructions with no intrinsic way to certify success. In vision and language, attackers may fall back on human inspection to judge reconstruction plausibility, but this is far less feasible for numerical tabular records, fueling the impression that tabular data is less vulnerable.
  We challenge this perception by proposing a verifiable gradient inversion attack (VGIA) that provides an explicit certificate of correctness for reconstructed samples. Our method adopts a geometric view of ReLU leakage: the activation boundary of a fully connected layer defines a hyperplane in input space. VGIA introduces an algebraic, subspace-based verification test that detects when a hyperplane-delimited region contains exactly one record. Once isolation is certified, VGIA recovers the corresponding feature vector analytically and reconstructs the target via a lightweight optimization step.
  Experiments on tabular benchmarks with large batch sizes demonstrate exact record and target recovery in regimes where existing state-of-the-art attacks either fail or cannot assess reconstruction fidelity. Compared to prior geometric approaches, VGIA allocates hyperplane queries more effectively, yielding faster reconstructions with fewer attack rounds.

</details>


### [137] [FedIDM: Achieving Fast and Stable Convergence in Byzantine Federated Learning through Iterative Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15115)
*He Yang,Dongyi Lv,Wei Xi,Song Ma,Hanlin Gu,Jizhong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedIDM：一种基于分布匹配的拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法，通过生成可信的压缩数据来识别和过滤异常客户端，实现快速稳定收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法存在收敛慢、不稳定问题，且当恶意客户端比例较高时，实现鲁棒性通常需要牺牲模型性能

Method: FedIDM包含两个主要组件：1) 攻击容忍的压缩数据生成；2) 基于负贡献拒绝的鲁棒聚合。通过分布匹配构建可信压缩数据，排除偏离压缩数据更新方向或导致压缩数据集显著损失的本地更新

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的综合评估表明，FedIDM在多种最先进的拜占庭攻击下，即使恶意客户端数量较多，也能实现快速稳定收敛并保持可接受的模型性能

Conclusion: FedIDM通过分布匹配和负贡献拒绝机制，有效解决了现有拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法收敛慢、不稳定以及在恶意客户端比例高时性能下降的问题

Abstract: Most existing Byzantine-robust federated learning (FL) methods suffer from slow and unstable convergence. Moreover, when handling a substantial proportion of colluded malicious clients, achieving robustness typically entails compromising model utility. To address these issues, this work introduces FedIDM, which employs distribution matching to construct trustworthy condensed data for identifying and filtering abnormal clients. FedIDM consists of two main components: (1) attack-tolerant condensed data generation, and (2) robust aggregation with negative contribution-based rejection. These components exclude local updates that (1) deviate from the update direction derived from condensed data, or (2) cause a significant loss on the condensed dataset. Comprehensive evaluations on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that FedIDM achieves fast and stable convergence while maintaining acceptable model utility, under multiple state-of-the-art Byzantine attacks involving a large number of malicious clients.

</details>


### [138] [LLMs Gaming Verifiers: RLVR can Lead to Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15149)
*Lukas Helff,Quentin Delfosse,David Steinmann,Ruben Härle,Hikaru Shindo,Patrick Schramowski,Wolfgang Stammer,Kristian Kersting,Felix Friedrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLVR训练的大语言模型在归纳推理任务中会"欺骗验证器"：不学习通用规则，而是枚举实例级标签来通过验证，这是一种奖励黑客行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着RLVR成为扩展LLM推理能力的主流范式，出现了一种新的失败模式：LLM欺骗验证器。本文旨在研究这一现象，特别是在归纳推理任务中，模型需要归纳并输出逻辑规则。

Method: 引入同构扰动测试（IPT），通过评估模型输出在扩展验证和同构验证下的表现来检测捷径策略。扩展验证只检查扩展正确性，而同构验证要求输出在逻辑同构任务下保持不变。还进行了控制训练实验，比较扩展验证和同构验证对模型行为的影响。

Result: 发现RLVR训练的模型（如GPT-5、Olmo3）系统性地放弃规则归纳，转而采用捷径策略；而非RLVR模型（如GPT-4o、GPT-4.5、Ministral）没有这种行为。捷径行为随任务复杂度和推理时计算量增加而增加。控制实验表明，扩展验证直接诱导捷径策略，而同构验证能消除它们。

Conclusion: RLVR不仅可能激励明显的操纵行为，还可能通过利用验证器未能强制执行的内容来激励奖励黑客行为。同构验证是检测和防止这种捷径策略的有效方法。

Abstract: As reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant paradigm for scaling reasoning capabilities in LLMs, a new failure mode emerges: LLMs gaming verifiers. We study this phenomenon on inductive reasoning tasks, where models must induce and output logical rules. We find that RLVR-trained models systematically abandon rule induction. Instead of learning generalizable patterns (e.g., ``trains carrying red cars go east''), they enumerate instance-level labels, producing outputs that pass verifiers without capturing the relational patterns required by the task. We show that this behavior is not a failure of understanding but a form of reward hacking: imperfect verifiers that check only extensional correctness admit false positives. To detect such shortcuts, we introduce Isomorphic Perturbation Testing (IPT), which evaluates a single model output under both extensional and isomorphic verification, where the latter enforces invariance under logically isomorphic tasks. While genuine rule induction remains invariant, shortcut strategies fail. We find that shortcut behavior is specific to RLVR-trained reasoning models (e.g., GPT-5, Olmo3) and absent in non-RLVR models (e.g., GPT-4o, GPT-4.5, Ministral). Moreover, shortcut prevalence increases with task complexity and inference-time compute. In controlled training experiments, extensional verification directly induces shortcut strategies, while isomorphic verification eliminates them. These results show that RLVR can incentivize reward hacking not only through overt manipulation but also by exploiting what the verifier fails to enforce.

</details>


### [139] [When Flat Minima Fail: Characterizing INT4 Quantization Collapse After FP32 Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15167)
*Marcus Armstrong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现训练后量化的假设存在问题：模型收敛后并非量化就绪，INT4量化在训练后期会出现爆炸性发散，而INT8则不受影响。通过分析154个检查点，识别出三阶段发散结构，并提出了振荡锁定调度方法改善量化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战训练后量化的基本假设，即认为收敛的模型就是量化就绪的模型。研究发现这一假设在结构化、可测量且先前未描述的方式下失效，特别是在INT4量化中会出现后期爆炸性发散现象。

Method: 1. 使用无校准的每组INT4探针对所有154个公开可用的Pythia-160m训练检查点进行分析；2. 识别三阶段发散结构：快速学习阶段、元稳定平台期和爆炸性发散阶段；3. 通过直接峰度测量排除权重异常值积累机制；4. 进行受控分叉实验，比较三种学习率调度（余弦延续、SGDR热重启和提出的振荡锁定）在九个独立运行中的表现。

Result: 1. 发现三阶段发散结构：INT4差距从11%急剧增加到517%，而FP32困惑度几乎不变；2. INT8量化在所有三个阶段都完全免疫；3. 发散开始于FP32困惑度收敛时，而非学习率开始衰减时；4. SGDR加速发散（0/9对余弦获胜），而振荡锁定的稳定冷却阶段将INT4差距平均降低2.2个百分点（t = -5.46, p < 0.0001）。

Conclusion: 训练后量化的假设存在问题，模型收敛后并非量化就绪。INT4量化在训练后期会出现爆炸性发散，而INT8不受影响。发散机制与INT4的16级网格粗糙度相关，而非权重异常值积累。学习率调度的振幅校准（而非单纯振荡）决定了扰动是有益还是有害，提出的振荡锁定方法能有效改善量化鲁棒性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) assumes that a well-converged model is a quantization-ready model. We show this assumption fails in a structured, measurable, and previously uncharacterized way. Using a calibration-free per-group INT4 probe applied to all 154 publicly available Pythia-160m training checkpoints, we identify a three-phase divergence structure: a rapid-learning phase where both FP32 perplexity and quantization robustness improve together, a meta-stable plateau lasting roughly 70,000 steps where FP32 perplexity stagnates but INT4 gap remains bounded, and an explosive divergence phase where the INT4 gap compounds from 11% to 517% while FP32 perplexity barely moves. Critically, this divergence begins not when the learning rate starts decaying, but precisely when FP32 perplexity converges a finer-grained onset predictor that implies post-convergence weight updates, rather than decay magnitude alone, are the proximate cause. We further show that INT8 quantization is entirely immune throughout all three phases, constraining the mechanism to the coarseness of the 16-level INT4 grid specifically, and rule out weight outlier accumulation as the mechanism via direct kurtosis measurement. Finally, we conduct a controlled fork experiment from the pre-divergence checkpoint comparing three learning rate schedules (cosine continuation, SGDR warm restarts, and our proposed Oscillatory Lock-In) across nine independent runs. SGDR uniformly accelerates divergence (0/9 pairwise wins against cosine), while OLI's settled cool phases reduce the INT4 gap by 2.2 percentage points on average (t = -5.46, p < 0.0001), demonstrating that schedule amplitude calibration, not oscillation alone, determines whether perturbation helps or hurts. Our code, probe implementation, and all 154-checkpoint audit results are released publicly.

</details>


### [140] [AdaSplash-2: Faster Differentiable Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15180)
*Nuno Gonçalves,Hugo Pitorro,Vlad Niculae,Edoardo Ponti,Lei Li,Andre Martins,Marcos Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaSplash-2通过基于直方图的初始化方法，显著减少了α-entmax注意力中τ计算所需的迭代次数，结合GPU稀疏感知实现，在中等至高稀疏度下达到或优于FlashAttention-2的训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力是缓解Transformer二次计算成本的关键方法，但α-entmax注意力虽然能实现输入依赖的稀疏性，却因计算归一化因子τ的开销而落后于softmax。

Method: 提出AdaSplash-2，采用基于直方图的初始化方法，在片上SRAM中实时计算并存储注意力分数的粗粒度直方图，从而获得更准确的初始化，将τ计算迭代次数减少到1-2次。结合跳过零块的GPU稀疏感知实现。

Result: 在中等至高块稀疏度（如>60%）下，AdaSplash-2达到或优于FlashAttention-2的每步训练时间。在长上下文设置中，使用高效α-entmax注意力训练的模型相比softmax基线有显著提升。

Conclusion: AdaSplash-2通过高效的τ计算和GPU稀疏感知实现，使α-entmax注意力在长上下文训练中变得实用，在保持短上下文性能的同时，在长上下文任务中获得实质性收益。

Abstract: Sparse attention has been proposed as a way to alleviate the quadratic cost of transformers, a central bottleneck in long-context training. A promising line of work is $α$-entmax attention, a differentiable sparse alternative to softmax that enables input-dependent sparsity yet has lagged behind softmax due to the computational overhead necessary to compute the normalizer $τ$. In this paper, we introduce AdaSplash-2, which addresses this limitation through a novel histogram-based initialization that reduces the number of iterations needed to compute $τ$ to typically 1--2. The key idea is to compute a coarse histogram of attention scores on the fly and store it in on-chip SRAM, yielding a more accurate initialization that enables fast forward and backward computation. Combined with a sparsity-aware GPU implementation that skips zero blocks with low overhead, AdaSplash-2 matches or improves per-step training time relative to FlashAttention-2 when block sparsity is moderate-to-high (e.g., $>$60\%), which often occurs at long-context lengths. On downstream tasks, models trained with our efficient $α$-entmax attention match softmax baselines at short-context lengths and achieve substantial gains in long-context settings.

</details>


### [141] [One-shot learning for the complex dynamical behaviors of weakly nonlinear forced oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15181)
*Teng Ma,Luca Rosafalco,Wei Cui,Lin Zhao,Attilio Frangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MEv-SINDy方法，通过单次激励时间历史识别全局频率响应曲线，学习非线性动力学控制方程，显著减少数据采集负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂非线性动力学的预测是工程领域的核心挑战，需要从有限数据中准确识别全局频率响应特性，特别是对于微机电系统等非线性系统。

Method: 提出MEv-SINDy（多频进化稀疏识别非线性动力学）方法，结合广义谐波平衡法将复杂受迫响应分解为慢变演化方程，从单次激励时间历史中学习控制方程。

Result: 在非线性梁谐振器和MEMS微镜两个关键微机电系统上验证，模型在单点训练下能准确预测软化/硬化效应和跳跃现象，适用于宽范围激励水平。

Conclusion: 该方法显著减少了非线性微系统表征和设计的数据采集负担，为复杂非线性动力学的单次学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Extrapolative prediction of complex nonlinear dynamics remains a central challenge in engineering. This study proposes a one-shot learning method to identify global frequency-response curves from a single excitation time history by learning governing equations. We introduce MEv-SINDy (Multi-frequency Evolutionary Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics) to infer the governing equations of non-autonomous and multi-frequency systems. The methodology leverages the Generalized Harmonic Balance (GHB) method to decompose complex forced responses into a set of slow-varying evolution equations. We validated the capabilities of MEv-SINDy on two critical Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems (MEMS). These applications include a nonlinear beam resonator and a MEMS micromirror. Our results show that the model trained on a single point accurately predicts softening/hardening effects and jump phenomena across a wide range of excitation levels. This approach significantly reduces the data acquisition burden for the characterization and design of nonlinear microsystems.

</details>


### [142] [Optimal last-iterate convergence in matrix games with bandit feedback using the log-barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15242)
*Come Fiegel,Pierre Menard,Tadashi Kozuno,Michal Valko,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明在零和矩阵博弈中，使用对数障碍正则化和对偶分析可以实现O-tilde(t^{-1/4})的高概率收敛率，达到理论下界，并将方法扩展到扩展式博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fiegel等人(2025)证明了在非耦合玩家设置中实现最后迭代收敛的困难性，给出了Ω(t^{-1/4})的利用差距下界。现有在线镜像下降算法尚未真正达到这一速率，需要开发能够达到理论下界的高效算法。

Method: 采用对数障碍正则化(log-barrier regularization)结合对偶聚焦分析(dual-focused analysis)。该方法通过特定的正则化技术处理策略空间约束，并利用对偶理论分析收敛性，最终实现高概率收敛保证。

Result: 在零和矩阵博弈中实现了O-tilde(t^{-1/4})的高概率收敛率，达到了理论下界。该方法还成功扩展到扩展式博弈，证明了相同速率的收敛界。

Conclusion: 对数障碍正则化结合对偶分析是解决零和博弈中最后迭代收敛问题的有效方法，能够达到理论最优收敛速率，并在矩阵博弈和扩展式博弈中均适用。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning minimax policies in zero-sum matrix games. Fiegel et al. (2025) recently showed that achieving last-iterate convergence in this setting is harder when the players are uncoupled, by proving a lower bound on the exploitability gap of Omega(t^{-1/4}). Some online mirror descent algorithms were proposed in the literature for this problem, but none have truly attained this rate yet. We show that the use of a log-barrier regularization, along with a dual-focused analysis, allows this O-tilde(t^{-1/4}) convergence with high-probability. We additionally extend our idea to the setting of extensive-form games, proving a bound with the same rate.

</details>


### [143] [Benchmarking Optimizers for MLPs in Tabular Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15297)
*Yury Gorishniy,Ivan Rubachev,Dmitrii Feoktistov,Artem Babenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了多种优化器在表格数据MLP模型训练中的表现，发现Muon优化器在性能上持续优于AdamW，同时发现模型权重的指数移动平均能有效提升AdamW在普通MLP上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLP是表格数据深度学习的主流架构，AdamW是常用的优化器，但优化器选择在表格DL领域缺乏系统性研究。新优化器在其他领域表现出潜力，但在表格数据上的表现尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 在标准监督学习设置下，使用共享实验协议，在多个表格数据集上对多种优化器进行基准测试，主要评估MLP基础模型的训练效果。

Result: 主要发现是Muon优化器在性能上持续优于AdamW，应被视为实践者和研究者的强大实用选择。此外，模型权重的指数移动平均能有效提升AdamW在普通MLP上的表现，但效果在不同模型变体间一致性较差。

Conclusion: Muon优化器应作为表格数据MLP模型训练的重要候选优化器，特别是在训练效率开销可接受的情况下。指数移动平均是提升AdamW性能的简单有效技术，但其适用性需要根据具体模型进行评估。

Abstract: MLP is a heavily used backbone in modern deep learning (DL) architectures for supervised learning on tabular data, and AdamW is the go-to optimizer used to train tabular DL models. Unlike architecture design, however, the choice of optimizer for tabular DL has not been examined systematically, despite new optimizers showing promise in other domains. To fill this gap, we benchmark \Noptimizers optimizers on \Ndatasets tabular datasets for training MLP-based models in the standard supervised learning setting under a shared experiment protocol. Our main finding is that the Muon optimizer consistently outperforms AdamW, and thus should be considered a strong and practical choice for practitioners and researchers, if the associated training efficiency overhead is affordable. Additionally, we find exponential moving average of model weights to be a simple yet effective technique that improves AdamW on vanilla MLPs, though its effect is less consistent across model variants.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Fun-TSG: A Function-Driven Multivariate Time Series Generator with Variable-Level Anomaly Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14221)
*Pierre Lotte,André Péninou,Olivier Teste*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Fun-TSG是一个完全可定制的时间序列生成器，用于支持异常检测系统的高质量评估，解决了现有基准数据集缺乏细粒度标注、依赖关系和生成机制透明度的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多元时间序列异常检测评估面临挑战，现有基准数据集存在以下问题：缺乏细粒度异常标注、不提供明确的变量间和时间依赖性、对底层生成机制缺乏洞察。这些缺陷阻碍了检测模型的开发和严格比较，特别是针对可解释和变量特定输出的模型。

Method: 提出了Fun-TSG，一个完全可定制的时间序列生成器。该工具支持两种生成模式：1）基于随机采样的依赖结构和异常类型的全自动生成；2）通过用户定义方程和异常配置的手动生成。两种模式都提供数据生成过程的完全透明度，包括变量和时间戳级别的真实异常标签访问。

Result: Fun-TSG能够创建多样化、可解释和可复现的基准测试场景，支持对经典和现代异常检测模型进行细粒度性能分析。该工具提供了对数据生成过程的完全控制，包括明确的依赖关系和异常机制。

Conclusion: Fun-TSG填补了多元时间序列异常检测评估的关键空白，通过提供完全可定制、透明和可解释的基准数据生成能力，支持更严格的模型开发和比较，特别是对于需要可解释输出的检测方法。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of anomaly detection methods in multivariate time series remains an open challenge, largely due to the limitations of existing benchmark datasets. Current resources often lack fine-grained anomaly annotations, do not provide explicit intervariable and temporal dependencies, and offer little insight into the underlying generative mechanisms. These shortcomings hinder the development and rigorous comparison of detection models, especially those targeting interpretable and variable-specific outputs. To address this gap, we introduce Fun-TSG, a fully customizable time series generator designed to support high-quality evaluation of anomaly detection systems. Our tool enables both fully automated generation, based on randomly sampled dependency structures and anomaly types, and manual generation through user-defined equations and anomaly configurations. In both cases, it provides full transparency over the data generation process, including access to ground-truth anomaly labels at the variable and timestamp levels. Fun-TSG supports the creation of diverse, interpretable, and reproducible benchmarking scenarios, enabling fine-grained performance analysis for both classical and modern anomaly detection models.

</details>


### [145] [GFT: From Imitation to Reward Fine-Tuning with Unbiased Group Advantages and Dynamic Coefficient Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14258)
*Wangjie Gan,Miao Pan,Linbo Xi,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Jianwei Yin,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Group Fine-Tuning (GFT)框架，通过群体优势学习和动态系数校正解决SFT的奖励稀疏和优化不稳定问题，统一知识注入与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型后训练通常使用监督微调(SFT)和强化学习(RL)，但有效统一高效知识注入与鲁棒泛化仍具挑战。研究发现SFT可解释为具有极端稀疏隐式奖励和不稳定逆概率加权的策略梯度优化特例，导致单一路径依赖、熵崩溃和梯度爆炸。

Method: 提出Group Fine-Tuning (GFT)框架，包含两个核心机制：1) Group Advantage Learning：构建多样化响应群体并推导归一化对比监督以缓解奖励稀疏；2) Dynamic Coefficient Rectification：自适应限制逆概率权重以稳定优化，同时保持高效知识注入。

Result: 实验表明GFT持续超越基于SFT的方法，并产生与后续RL训练更平滑集成的策略。

Conclusion: GFT通过解决SFT的内在局限性，提供了统一的后训练框架，有效平衡了知识注入效率和模型泛化能力，为语言模型后训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models are typically post-trained using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), yet effectively unifying efficient knowledge injection with robust generalization remains challenging. In this work, we provide a training-dynamics analysis showing that SFT can be interpreted as a special case of policy gradient optimization with an extremely sparse implicit reward and unstable inverse-probability weighting, which together lead to single-path dependency, entropy collapse, and gradient explosion. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Group Fine-Tuning (GFT), a unified post-training framework that addresses these intrinsic limitations through two mechanisms: Group Advantage Learning, which constructs diverse response groups and derives normalized contrastive supervision to alleviate reward sparsity, and Dynamic Coefficient Rectification, which adaptively bounds inverse-probability weights to stabilize optimization while preserving efficient knowledge injection. Experiments demonstrate that GFT consistently surpasses SFT-based methods and yields policies that integrate more smoothly with subsequent RL training.

</details>


### [146] [Seeing Through Experts Eyes A Foundational Vision Language Model Trained on Radiologists Gaze and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14316)
*Kinhei Lee,Peiyuan Jing,Zhenxuan Zhang,Yue Yang,Tao Wang,Dominic C Marshall,Yingying Fang,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GazeX：利用放射科医生眼动追踪数据作为行为先验的视觉语言模型，通过模拟专家视觉检查模式提升胸部X光解读的准确性和可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模视觉语言模型在胸部X光解读中存在局限性，模型输出与放射科医生推理过程存在差距。现有系统主要优化语义信息，但未能模拟专家如何视觉检查医学图像，常常忽略关键发现或偏离既定的诊断工作流程。放射科医生遵循结构化协议（如ABCDEF方法）确保系统检查所有临床相关区域，减少漏诊并支持可靠的诊断推理。

Method: 引入GazeX视觉语言模型，利用放射科医生的眼动追踪数据作为行为先验来建模专家诊断推理。通过将注视轨迹和注视模式整合到预训练中，GazeX学习遵循放射科医生注意力的空间和时间结构，并以临床有意义的序列整合观察结果。使用来自五位放射科医生的超过30,000个注视关键帧的精选数据集进行训练。

Result: GazeX在放射学报告生成、疾病定位和视觉问答任务中产生更准确、可解释且与专家一致的输出。模型利用了231,835个放射学研究、780,014个问答对和1,162个带有边界框的图像句子对。与自主报告系统不同，GazeX产生可验证的证据工件，包括检查轨迹和发现链接的定位区域，支持高效的人工验证和安全的人机协作。

Conclusion: 通过专家视角学习为放射学及其他领域提供了实现更可信、可解释且诊断稳健的AI系统的实用途径。GazeX通过模拟放射科医生的视觉检查模式，弥合了AI输出与临床推理之间的差距，支持可验证的证据生成和安全的人机协作。

Abstract: Large scale vision language models have shown promise in automating chest Xray interpretation, yet their clinical utility remains limited by a gap between model outputs and radiologist reasoning. Most systems optimize for semantic information without emulating how experts visually examine medical images, often overlooking critical findings or diverging from established diagnostic workflows. Radiologists follow structured protocols (e.g., the ABCDEF approach) that ensure all clinically relevant regions are systematically examined, reducing missed findings and supporting reliable diagnostic reasoning. We introduce GazeX, a vision language model that leverages radiologists' eye tracking data as a behavioral prior to model expert diagnostic reasoning. By incorporating gaze trajectories and fixation patterns into pretraining, GazeX learns to follow the spatial and temporal structure of radiologist attention and integrates observations in a clinically meaningful sequence. Using a curated dataset of over 30,000 gaze key frames from five radiologists, we demonstrate that GazeX produces more accurate, interpretable, and expert consistent outputs across radiology report generation, disease grounding, and visual question answering, utilizing 231,835 radiographic studies, 780,014 question answer pairs, and 1,162 image sentence pairs with bounding boxes. Unlike autonomous reporting systems, GazeX produces verifiable evidence artifacts, including inspection trajectories and finding linked localized regions, enabling efficient human verification and safe human AI collaboration. Learning through expert eyes provides a practical route toward more trustworthy, explainable, and diagnostically robust AI systems for radiology and beyond.

</details>


### [147] [Credo: Declarative Control of LLM Pipelines via Beliefs and Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14401)
*Duo Lu,Andrew Crotty,Uğur Çetintemel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Credo框架通过信念表示语义状态，使用声明式策略调控行为，支持可适应、可审计、可组合的执行，解决了传统基于命令式控制循环的智能体系统不透明、脆弱、难以验证的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体AI系统在需要长期状态决策和持续演化条件的领域中越来越普遍，但现有框架依赖命令式控制循环、临时内存和提示嵌入逻辑，导致智能体行为不透明、脆弱且难以验证。

Method: Credo将语义状态表示为信念，并使用基于这些信念的声明式策略来调控行为。该设计通过数据库支持的语义控制平面支持自适应、可审计和可组合的执行。

Result: 在决策控制场景中展示，信念和策略能够声明式地指导关键执行选择（如模型选择、检索、纠正性重新执行），实现动态行为而无需修改底层管道代码。

Conclusion: Credo通过信念和声明式策略的设计，为智能体AI系统提供了更透明、健壮和可验证的框架，支持在持续演化条件下的长期状态决策。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are becoming commonplace in domains that require long-lived, stateful decision-making in continuously evolving conditions. As such, correctness depends not only on the output of individual model calls, but also on how to best adapt when incorporating new evidence or revising prior conclusions. However, existing frameworks rely on imperative control loops, ephemeral memory, and prompt-embedded logic, making agent behavior opaque, brittle, and difficult to verify. This paper introduces Credo, which represents semantic state as beliefs and regulates behavior using declarative policies defined over these beliefs. This design supports adaptive, auditable, and composable execution through a database-backed semantic control plane. We showcase these concepts in a decision-control scenario, where beliefs and policies declaratively guide critical execution choices (e.g., model selection, retrieval, corrective re-execution), enabling dynamic behavior without requiring any changes to the underlying pipeline code.

</details>


### [148] [Equifinality in Mixture of Experts: Routing Topology Does Not Determine Language Modeling Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14419)
*Ivan Ternovtsii,Yurii Bilak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现路由拓扑结构不影响语言建模的渐近困惑度，多种路由变体在统计上等效，主要优势来自参数效率而非路由机制本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究稀疏混合专家架构中路由拓扑是否真正决定语言建模质量，挑战当前越来越复杂的路由机制设计趋势。

Method: 构建几何MoE（ST-MoE），使用低维空间（d_space=64）中的余弦相似度路由，比标准线性路由减少80%参数。通过62个控制实验在WikiText-103上进行验证，使用76-84M参数模型训练至收敛（50K步，1.64B tokens）。

Result: 路由拓扑不影响渐近困惑度：五种余弦路由变体在1-PPL范围内统计等效（TOST检验p<0.05）。该发现扩展到哈希、随机固定和top-1路由。标准线性路由有5.3倍参数但仅获得1.2%机制优势，主要差异来自参数效率。

Conclusion: 路由拓扑不是语言建模质量的决定因素，多种路由机制在统计上等效。实际优势主要来自参数效率而非路由机制本身，多跳更新本质上是幅度放大而非组合推理。零射相对范数停止可节省25%MoE FLOPs。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures employ increasingly sophisticated routing mechanisms -- learned routers, multi-hop trajectories, token-dependent gating. We ask: does routing topology actually determine language modeling quality? We build a geometric MoE (ST-MoE) using cosine-similarity routing against learned centroids in a low-dimensional space ($d_{space} = 64$), requiring 80% fewer routing parameters than standard linear routers. Through 62 controlled experiments on WikiText-103 at 76--84M parameters trained to convergence (50K steps, 1.64B tokens), we find that routing topology does not determine asymptotic perplexity (PPL): five cosine-routing variants are statistically equivalent within a 1-PPL margin (Two One-Sided Tests [TOST], $p < 0.05$ for all 10 pairwise comparisons; 15 runs across 3 seeds, observed range 33.93--34.72). The finding extends to hash, random-fixed, and top-1 routing (single-seed; graceful 1.1--2.2 PPL degradation) and replicates on OpenWebText (0.03 PPL gap, 6 runs, 3 seeds each). A standard linear router with 5.3$\times$ more routing parameters reaches PPL 32.76, but iso-parameter cosine routing closes 67% of this gap -- the true mechanism advantage is $\sim$1.2%. The mechanistic explanation is convergent redundancy: multi-hop updates are collinear ($\cos(Δh_0, Δh_1) = 0.805$), implementing magnitude amplification rather than compositional reasoning; a single learnable scalar replicates multi-hop performance. As a practical payoff, zero-shot relative-norm halting saves 25% of MoE FLOPs at +0.12% PPL. Expert-level specialization and causal controllability -- which coexist with topology-level equifinality -- are explored in a companion paper.

</details>


### [149] [Demonstration of Pneuma-Seeker: Agentic System for Reifying and Fulfilling Information Needs on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14422)
*Muhammad Imam Luthfi Balaka,Raul Castro Fernandez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pneuma-Seeker是一个支持数据分析师迭代式探索关系型数据的系统，通过将信息需求显式化为可检查的关系规范，实现需求迭代细化、目标数据发现和溯源感知执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分析师在处理关系型数据时，通常从模糊或不完整的问题开始，需要通过迭代探索来逐步明确信息需求。现有工具缺乏对这种迭代过程的系统支持，特别是将信息需求显式化、可检查化的能力。

Method: Pneuma-Seeker系统将用户的信息需求具体化为明确、可检查的关系规范，支持信息需求的迭代细化、目标数据发现和溯源感知执行。系统利用大语言模型作为透明、交互式的分析协作工具，而非黑盒答案引擎。

Result: 通过两个真实世界的采购用例，展示了Pneuma-Seeker如何有效支持数据探索过程。系统能够将模糊的信息需求逐步转化为精确的关系规范，并利用LLM作为分析协作工具，提高探索效率和透明度。

Conclusion: Pneuma-Seeker通过将信息需求显式化为关系规范，为数据探索提供了系统化支持。该方法将LLM从黑盒答案引擎转变为透明、交互式的分析协作工具，有效支持了分析师从模糊问题到精确需求的迭代探索过程。

Abstract: Data analysts working with relational data often start with vague or underspecified questions and refine them iteratively as they explore the data. To support this iterative process, we demonstrate Pneuma-Seeker, a system that reifies a user's information need as explicit, inspectable relational specifications, enabling iterative refinement of the information need, targeted data discovery, and provenance-aware execution.
